  959|       |void cache_stats::print_stats(FILE *fout, unsigned long long streamID,
  960|      0|                              const char *cache_name) const {
  961|       |  ///
  962|       |  /// For a given CUDA stream, print out each non-zero cache statistic for every
  963|       |  /// memory access type and status "cache_name" defaults to "Cache_stats" when
  964|       |  /// no argument is provided, otherwise the provided name is used. The printed

 1847|       |/// This is meant to model the first level data cache in Fermi.
 1848|       |/// It is write-evict (global) or write-back (local) at
 1849|       |/// the granularity of individual blocks
 1850|       |/// (the policy used in fermi according to the CUDA manual)
 1851|       |class l1_cache : public data_cache {
 1852|       | public:

 1855|       |           enum mem_fetch_status status, class gpgpu_sim *gpu,
 1856|       |           enum cache_gpu_level level)
 1857|     37|      : data_cache(name, config, core_id, type_id, memport, mfcreator, status,
 1858|     37|                   L1_WR_ALLOC_R, L1_WRBK_ACC, gpu, level) {}
 1859|       |
 1860|     37|  virtual ~l1_cache() {}
 1861|       |
 1862|       |  virtual enum cache_request_status access(new_addr_type addr, mem_fetch *mf,
 1863|       |                                           unsigned time,
 1864|       |                                           std::list<cache_event> &events);
 1865|       |
 1866|       | protected:
 1867|       |  l1_cache(const char *name, cache_config &config, int core_id, int type_id,
 1868|       |           mem_fetch_interface *memport, mem_fetch_allocator *mfcreator,
 1869|       |           enum mem_fetch_status status, tag_array *new_tag_array,
 1870|       |           class gpgpu_sim *gpu)
 1871|       |      : data_cache(name, config, core_id, type_id, memport, mfcreator, status,
 1872|      0|                   new_tag_array, L1_WR_ALLOC_R, L1_WRBK_ACC, gpu) {}
 1873|       |};
 1874|       |

 1922|       |  /// Access function for tex_cache
 1923|       |  /// return values: RESERVATION_FAIL if request could not be accepted
 1924|       |  /// otherwise returns HIT_RESERVED or MISS; NOTE: *never* returns HIT
